34:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"NL generation and load: sourcing basis"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":["The Netherlands hourly generation per production type and actual total load come from the ENTSO-E Transparency Platform (document types ","A75"," and"," ","A65",", process type ","A16",", bidding zone EIC"," ","10YNL----------L","), retrieved daily by the snapshot job and once for the full-history backfill. ENTSO-E timestamps are UTC interval starts; the pipeline converts them explicitly to Europe/Amsterdam local time through the same slot keying as every other series, and every build verifies the conversion hour-by-hour against the unchanged energy-charts price series. Renewable shares are computed from the per-type feeds: the renewable set is distribution-connected renewables (psrType B16 Solar plus B20 Other renewable), wind onshore, wind offshore, biomass, hydro run-of-river and poundage, hydro water reservoir, hydro pumped storage (generation side), geothermal and waste, over total generation for the share of generation and over actual load for the share of load."]}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":["Dutch solar and other distribution-connected renewables are reported by the TSO as a single upscaled estimate (ENTSO-E psrType B20 Other renewable plus B16 Solar); the site presents that aggregate rather than a decomposition, and the renewable share is unaffected because every component is renewable. The probe gate recorded an aggregate peak of ","9.8"," GW on the 2026-06-15 midday window, about ","26.9","% of the ","36.46"," GW daylight-connected fleet (energy-charts installed_power, latest record: solar AC plus onshore wind), an annualized aggregate of ","39.2"," TWh per year, and a midday-to-night mean ratio of ","3.9"," across the trailing year."]}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"Fetch integrity: the platform serves each production type in two coexisting published blocks, so the fetch layer deduplicates per type by block completeness (the more complete block wins, a structural preference, never a value comparison) and verifies every accepted window against its load document: total generation energy must sit between 0.6 and 1.4 times the window's load energy (Dutch generation has been roughly 85–105% of load, per CBS), re-fetching the window fresh up to 3 times otherwise. These are fetch-integrity constants of the data pipeline, not display thresholds. An earlier probe run drew a defective platform variant of the same window; that run's figures are not shown here."}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"Historical note: the energy-charts NL solar series was defective (detected November 2026). It yielded about 0.48 TWh of solar per year against a 29.28 GW installed fleet (the same source's installed_power endpoint), while CBS reports 25.52 billion kWh of Dutch solar production in 2025 (provisional). Fixed in November 2026 by switching NL generation and load to ENTSO-E per-type data. The full probe report and the cross-zone solar audit are recorded in the pull request description."}]]}]
35:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"Basis disclosure (verbatim)"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":["Computed carbon intensity uses the ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"generation mix"}],": domestic production types times the emission-factor table, divided by load. ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"Imports are not attributed"}],": cross-border flows carry no fuel mix, so import-heavy hours show a lower computed intensity than the power actually consumed would cause. This limitation applies everywhere intensity is shown (the intensity layer label, the green window card captions, the coda)."]}]]}]
f8:T559,The green window is computed from the trailing 30 days of hourly values (price and renewable share of generation), each hour independent. A green hour has price at or below the 30th percentile of the trailing price distribution AND renewable share at or above the 70th percentile of the trailing renewable-share distribution. A red hour is the exact inverse through the same code path: price at or above the 70th percentile AND renewable share at or below the 30th percentile. The rendered window is the hour interval (at least two contiguous wall-clock hours) that recurs most often as a whole: the number of trailing days on which every hour of the interval qualifies under the rule above. Ties prefer the longer interval, then the earliest start. Its frequency share is the share of trailing days on which the full interval qualifies. Hours qualifying outside the window still count in the frequency strip and the risk indicators individually. Percentiles use linear interpolation on the sorted hourly sample. Basis: renewable share of generation (NL generation mix); price is the NL day-ahead price. The strip can also mark one other recurring window: the strongest interval that qualifies on at least half as many days as the window and is separated from it by at least one hour. It is drawn fainter than the window and named with its opening share in the caption.36:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"The green window rule (verbatim)"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"$f8"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1 text-xs","children":["Constants: trailing ",30," days, green price ≤ p",30," / renewables ≥ p",70,", red price ≥ p",70," / renewables ≤ p",30,". They live only in src/grid/green-window.ts and change only by editing that file; any change is stated in the PR description."]}]]}]

3a:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"The atlas rhythm grids"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":["The atlas card renders month-by-hour rhythm grids over the trailing window (","12 November 2025"," to"," ","19 November 2026",","," ",373," days): each cell is the mean of that local hour across all days of that calendar month present in the trailing window, computed at build time from the snapshot's trailing days. The slot-to-hour mapping uses the existing DST wall-hour sequence, so the 23-hour spring-forward day contributes no 02:00 sample and the 25-hour fall-back day contributes both of its 02:00 slots to the 02:00 cell (wall-hour collapse; nothing is silently shifted). Months are calendar months across the window, and the two edge months are partial, cut by the window's first and last day. Colour domains are the min and max of each grid's own cells, computed at build time: the price scale positions values by the square root of the domain position so spike hours do not wash out the normal range, the renewable-share scale is linear, and the legend ticks are rounded values computed from that domain."]}]]}]
3b:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"Suppression and gap rendering"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"Missing or suppressed source values are stored as null and rendered as grey gap cells with a \"no data\" label, never as zero, never interpolated. The rhythm-grid colour scales span the min to max of each grid's own cells, computed at build time; the legend prints rounded tick values chosen from that domain."}]]}]
3c:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"Chart axes"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"Every bar chart carries one left y-axis computed at build time from its own series: zero sits at the baseline, the top tick is the series maximum rounded up to a human step from the 1, 2, 5 family, with three to five tick values, and the unit is stated once per axis (MW for the day profiles, GW for the flow timelines, TWh for the volume bars). The overlay line in the day profiles is scaled to its own maximum, so it deliberately does not share the bar axis; its exact values appear in the tooltip on hover, keyboard focus or tap."}]]}]
3d:["$","section",null,{"children":[["$","h3",null,{"className":"font-semibold text-foreground","children":"Chart interaction"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-1","children":"One shared tooltip (a single client component, components/chart-tooltip.tsx) follows the cursor over every chart and snaps to the nearest datum. On keyboard focus of a chart element the tooltip appears anchored to that element; on touch devices a tap positions it at the tapped datum and marks it active, a tap on another datum moves it, and a tap outside the chart or any scroll dismisses it. The tooltip is aria-hidden and visual only; every chart element keeps its own aria-label as the screen-reader channel. Hover and touch emphasis is stroke-only, so no interaction ever changes the layout."}]]}]
